# Aletheia answer

**Request:** Generate analogues of erlotinib (C#Cc1cccc(Nc2ncnc3cc(OCCOC)c(OCCOC)cc23)c1) for EGFR with better predicted solubility and lower synthetic complexity, then rank them.

*run `run-20261113T005843-9f5be8` · source `mixed` · 4.4s · planner `callable+cache / blackboard` · intents generate_analogs, property_profile, rank_candidates*
